- September 4: Sugar53% and Malaysia42% resolved with positive last-forecast skill; Parrott and Golden Global were unlisted. Parrott actually came off the bench while Cucho started; no demonstrated lineup error. API threshold/state request is distinct from the national emergency act. Small banks and substitute scorers need candidate/field coverage without hindsight certainty. See memory/calibration_note_2026-09-04.md.

- Lack of a current announcement is weak evidence for no event during a future target window. Four resolved top-nonoccurrence forecasts(58/65/91/84%) all missed in this selected pool; use context and new procedural evidence, not a universal confidence cap. See memory/calibration_note_2026-09-02.md. The named Gaza plan outcome is Ben-Gvir per harness; retrieved Katz comments do not independently verify that identity.

- For deadline record questions, a completed current leader is not a final winner. September1 Enzo125m beat Barcola despite yesterday Barcola81/Enzo9; a live near140m-euro agreement had already been reported. Keep rival completion and comparable-fee scenarios explicit through the deadline. See memory/calibration_note_2026-09-01.md.

- Read the full resolution criteria. A transfer question may be conditional on a transfer, while a mixed-doubles question may explicitly resolve to no opposing pair when the proposed pairing never competes. Treat these differently.
- With at most five outcomes, keep actual estimated probabilities and leave residual mass for unlisted real possibilities. Do not force five guesses to total 100% in a large field.
- Search evidence is date capped, but an event URL containing 2026 does not establish that a displayed results table is from 2026. Some results pages show the previous year's event. Check article dates and the described event.
- Do not infer a future typhoon's track from a previous storm using the same recurring name. Before a storm forms, use broad geographic climatology.
- For World Cup quarterfinals and semifinals, use the official bracket, group-position probabilities, and current results. Generic team-title odds are insufficient for a particular branch of the draw.
- For distant, narrow identity questions without direct reporting, disperse probabilities across plausible real candidates and retain substantial residual mass.
- For event winners, recheck the actual published entry field and injury withdrawals when the field is announced. A defending champion or top-ranked athlete is not automatically entered; official event pages can still display last year. Track field announcements for distant events rather than waiting for mainstream daily headlines.
- For a record-holder question, search explicitly for announced challengers before assigning near certainty to the incumbent. The SK Hynix IPO omission was costly: broad local financial headlines revealed a specific rival missed by the initial search. Read the exact benchmark, dates and whether pricing alone qualifies.

- For political succession questions, explicitly search the complete current candidate field and inspect alternative names in full reports. A prominent incumbent or acting appointment can crowd out a viable technocrat; interim service is not evidence of a confirmed permanent nomination.
- In award and event fields, distinguish elected/nominated from participating. A listed All-Star starter may already be injured and unavailable, while an actor nomination does not imply the show was nominated for series.

- Refresh transfer favorites periodically even when no headline names the player: rare-name body scans can reveal rival talks inside another player's transfer report. High probabilities require current support; an old rival-withdrawal report can quickly become stale.
- A specific preliminary match-time guide is weaker than the published order of play. Keep schedule uncertainty until the day-before order is available, especially for first-completed-match questions.

## June 24 evidence to retain

- F1: Mercedes won the first six races. Kimi Antonelli leads on 156 points despite retiring from the June 14 Barcelona race; Hamilton won for Ferrari and has 115, Russell 106. Older assumptions about McLaren dominance are inappropriate.
- Wimbledon: Carlos Alcaraz withdrew with a wrist injury. Sinner is a strong men's favorite. The women's field is open: Sabalenka recent deciding-set bagels; Rybakina a hip concern; Swiatek no final since September; Andreeva won Roland-Garros.
- World Cup: Messi has five goals; Mbappe and Haaland four. June 24 markets put France near 19–20%, Argentina 15–16%, Spain 14–15%, England 11–12%, Portugal around 8%. Argentina's projected knockout path is comparatively favorable.
- Women's T20: England 4–0 and qualified; Australia 4–0 and dominant. India must recover after losing to South Africa.
- US trade: April's initial report put Taiwan and Vietnam deficits both near $19.3bn, ahead of Mexico/China. Taiwan leads year to date.
- Data-center moratorium: Maine governor vetoed April measure. New York legislature passed one June 4; Hochul's signature remains uncertain.
- Javelin: Rumesh Tharanga Pathirage threw 92.62m in Rome, and Yan Ziyi 71.74m in Xiamen; these current performances materially change older priors.
- US–Iran: June 17 MOU starts a 60-day negotiation period. US–Houthi ceasefire remained in place in June reporting; a specific one-week US strike question should retain substantial no-event probability.

## General daily update priorities

- Read the new day's local articles and current market snapshot first; retain existing forecasts unless new evidence or a discovered error warrants an update.
- World Cup final group matches and Women's T20 results can quickly alter bracket and award probabilities.
- Monitor Jaylen Brown trade negotiations, early NBA free-agency announcements, and football transfers for named agreements rather than recycled rumor lists.
- Refresh the nearest-term political appointments and narrow scheduled announcements when direct evidence appears.
- Preserve scarce evidence for poorly covered questions; do not turn an unsupported possibility into a confident prediction merely to fill the outcome limit.
